# M0 integration log

Branch `m0-integrate`, started from `m0` at `b4bca76` on 13 October 2026. It combines the latest `main` and the eight Stage 0 gate branches, so parallel M1 work can start from one tree: seven on 13 October 2026, once their final reviews were complete, and G0.4 audio on 14 October 2026, after its final review (a second integration agent). Integration by agents; every outcome below is agent-reviewed, human sign-off pending. On 14-15 October 2026 an assembly agent merged the thirteen M0 items that followed (the remaining review findings of each gate, the contracts, the dependency lock, the threat model, the device runbook and the plan amendments), applied their shared-file requests and ran the complete CI from a clean tree: section 7. Cells below that the assembly changed say so. On 15 October 2026 the product owner merged `m0-integrate` (at `177ed9a`) into `main` through pull request #4, before the M0 gate was approved; the later M0 work continues on this branch. On 19 October 2026 an agent adopted Git LFS for the snapshot references and evidence images, on the product owner's decision of 13 October 2026: section 9. The gate summary was then refreshed: section 10. Sections 11 and 12 record the M0 final fix and its second round (19 and 20 October 2026), and section 13 the toolchain re-pin to Xcode 27.1 on 20 October 2026, PO-35 as ADR-0001 records the reading of the product owner's reply, with the answers to the final review's third round; section 14 the review of that round and its fix, the same day. Pull request #5 then merged this branch into `main` (merge commit `4c73da57`). On 22 October 2026 the product owner decided every M0 question, and an agent recorded the decisions in the Stage 0 records on a branch made from that merge: section 15.

**G0.4 audio was merged a day later.** On 13 October `m0-g04-audio` was still in a fix-and-review round, so the first integration left it out and marked it as later in every index. The second integration squash-merged it on 14 October 2026 (section 2, order 8), applied or recorded its shared-file requests (section 3, rows 25 to 31), checked its binary files (section 4) and ran its CI drop-in and the contract checks from a clean tree (section 5). Text written at the first integration that G0.4 changed is updated in place, with the date.

## 1. Merge of `main`

`git merge --no-ff main` (merge commit `cf88104`). `main` carried three changes `m0` lacked:

| Change | Outcome |
| --- | --- |
| `Docs/Sylvia_Companion_Vision_Architecture_Addendum.md` (proposed addendum, 12 October 2026) | Brought in unchanged. Its suggestions (including its Section 12 roadmap addition) are not applied to the plan. |
| `Docs/Sylvia_Product_Brief.md`: "Later - one home for everything you read and hear" paragraph | Merged without conflict. |
| Plan: unified content hub roadmap in Section 24, a Section 3.3 sentence, D14, front matter and an Appendix D entry, labelled "draft 0.3" on `main` | Merged; see below. |

The plan had two textual conflicts (front matter; the D14/D15 rows) and one semantic one (both sides call their revision "draft 0.3"). Resolution, keeping every change from both sides:

- `m0`'s pre-M0 decision revision stays **draft 0.3** (6 October).
- `main`'s roadmap revision becomes **Draft 0.4 - 7 October 2026** in Appendix D, same bullets, with one sentence saying it was written on `main` as 0.3 and renumbered at integration. Appendix D lists revisions newest first (as `m0` already did: 0.3 above 0.2), so 0.4 is the first entry; `main` had appended its entry at the end of the file.
- Front matter: status "Draft 0.4", revised 7 October 2026, naming drafts 0.2, 0.3 and 0.4.
- D14 takes `main`'s wording (articles/feeds/newsletters, phases A-C, the Phase A versus Docker ordering decision). D15 keeps `m0`'s wording (seven-marker set, opaque chrome); `main`'s D15 was the unchanged draft 0.2 row.
- Section 3.3 and Section 24 merged automatically. A diff of the result against each parent shows every `m0` change and every `main` change present once.

## 2. Gate branches (squash merges)

Each branch was merged with `git merge --squash <branch>` in the order below. No merge produced a conflict: the seven branches add 1,822 files and modify none that existed on `m0`, and no file is touched by two branches. After the seventh merge a script compared every path each branch changes (relative to its merge base `77f0be4`) with the integration tree: all 1,822 blobs are identical to the branch's final commit.

**G0.4 (14 October 2026).** `git merge --squash m0-g04-audio` on top of `b354dc7` produced no conflict: relative to its merge base `77f0be4` the branch adds 131 files and modifies none, and none of the 131 paths existed on `m0-integrate`. Before committing, every path in the index was compared with the branch head (`git ls-files -s` against `git ls-tree -r m0-g04-audio`): all 131 identical in mode and blob. The squash commit names the branch, its final commit, the review verdict and the report.

Each branch head equalled the final commit its last review examined, so no unreviewed commit was merged (G0.4: `m0-g04-audio` was at `459229347684220687b814a162d2fcdd863c718c`, the commit review 6 examined, when it was merged). The open findings are minor and stay with their gates; they are listed in each review and are not fixed by this integration.

The review findings stay open except one: g01-epub R3-02 ("committed generated EPUB fixtures are missing from `Fixtures/LICENSES.md`, which says generated fixtures are never committed") is resolved by the `Fixtures/LICENSES.md` update below.

## 4. Binary files and Git LFS

*(19 October 2026: the deferral below was replaced by the product owner's decision of 13 October 2026 to adopt Git LFS, carried out in section 9.)*

**Decision: plain git for now; LFS deferred, not rejected.** Recorded with the measurements, the reason, the trigger and the exact steps in ADR-0002 ("Binary test baselines and evidence images"). In short: all eight gate branches (G0.4 read with git at `943d5e778de2b58f47343f0e146f9e3ed7baa70e`, still in review) add 786 binary files, 65,265,478 bytes, of which 762 PNG (64,437,948 bytes, largest 450,701): 527 snapshot references and 235 evidence images. git-lfs 3.7.1 is installed on the build Mac but no LFS filter is configured at any level, so adopting LFS now would leave every worktree on this Mac, including the clean verification run that follows integration, with pointer files instead of PNGs until the owner runs `git lfs install`. Measurements: `evidence/M0-Integration/binary-files.tsv` and `binary-files-summary.txt` (command `evidence/M0-Integration/commands/measure.sh`). No `.gitattributes` was added and no remote operation was run.

**At the G0.4 merge (14 October 2026) the decision stands, and there was nothing to renormalize.** The repository still has no `.gitattributes`, and no `filter.lfs` setting exists at any level (`git config --show-origin --get-regexp 'filter\.lfs|lfs\.'` finds only `lfs.repositoryformatversion 0` in the shared repository configuration), so G0.4's binaries are plain git like every other gate's. G0.4's final commit adds 12 evidence images (1,259,167 bytes, largest 222,609), 6 more than were read at `943d5e7`, and no other binary file; each begins with the PNG signature (no LFS pointer) and has no git attribute, and all lie under `Docs/Stage0/evidence/G04-Audio/results/`, which the ADR-0002 evidence pattern covers. The eight gates now add 792 binary files (66,082,214 bytes), 768 of them PNG (65,254,684 bytes) and 241 of those evidence images; ADR-0002 is updated with these numbers (`evidence/M0-Integration/g04-binary-files.txt`, command `commands/g04-binary-files.sh`).

## 5. Checks run at integration

Fast checks only; the complete CI from a clean tree is run on the final commit by a separate verification agent. Host: macOS 26.6.2 (25G83), Apple M5 Max, Xcode 27.0 (27A266a), XcodeGen 2.46.0. No simulator was created or used.

| Check | Command | Result | Evidence |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Contracts | `Scripts/ci.sh contracts` | pass: 15 schemas, 188 fixtures, 1 OpenAPI document, 1 instance | `evidence/M0-Integration/contracts.log` |
| Package locks | `evidence/M0-Integration/commands/lockcheck.sh`: `sylvia_generate` and `xcodebuild -resolvePackageDependencies -disableAutomaticPackageResolution` for the 7 Xcode projects; `swift package resolve --force-resolved-versions` for the 10 Swift packages | pass: all 17 resolve and no committed `Package.resolved` changes. Two projects have no committed lock (below). `ZipEval/IdentityProbe` is not resolved: it is G0.3's probe that two packages named ZIPFoundation cannot share a graph | `evidence/M0-Integration/package-locks.log` |
| Licenses | `evidence/M0-Integration/commands/license_inventory.py` over the checkouts the lock check left | 23 of 23 pinned packages found at their pinned revision; licenses in ADR-0014 | `evidence/M0-Integration/license-inventory.log` |
| Squash fidelity | compare every path each branch changes with the integration tree (blob IDs) | 1,822 of 1,822 identical | section 2 |

**At the G0.4 merge (14 October 2026)** the second integration ran G0.4's CI drop-in and the contract checks from a clean tree on the squash commit `269accf` (no uncommitted change): every DerivedData directory and the CI outputs in the worktree were deleted first (there were none), then `Scripts/fixtures/fetch.sh` and the three G0.4 generators ran (all fixtures current), then `Scripts/ci.sh contracts` and `Scripts/ci.sh g04`. The drop-in ran on this integration's own simulator `Sylvia-M0-integrate-late` (iPhone 17, iOS 26.5; created by the drop-in with `sylvia_sim_udid`, shut down by it on exit and deleted afterwards) with its default settings (`G04_RESULTS_DIR`, `G04_DEVICE_FIXTURES`, `G04_SKIP_REALAV` and `G04_KEEP_SIM` unset), so its result tables went to `.build/ci-results/` and did not replace G0.4's committed ones, and the 8.6 GB device bundle with the fixtures was not rebuilt (G0.4's clean run did that: `G04-Audio/results/device-build-fixtures.txt`). Every player ran at test volume (1 %; `SYLVIA_AUDIBLE_PLAYBACK` unset), so every measurement in the run was taken at test volume. Other agents' jobs shared the Mac: the 1-minute load average was 7.99 at the start and 7.77 at the end, and 5.1-69.1 in the drop-in's 30-second samples, with 1-2 simulators booted (`evidence/M0-Integration/ci-g04-after-merge-host-load.tsv`, a copy of the drop-in's `host-load.tsv`), so the run's timings are indicative only. The commits after `269accf` change only documentation and evidence (`git diff 269accf HEAD -- Spikes Scripts Packages Apps Contracts` lists only `Contracts/README.md`).

## 6. Open items from integration

- **Owner:** run `git lfs install` on each Mac that builds Sylvia, then adopt LFS per ADR-0002 before the first snapshot re-record or the first push of `m0`. **Done on 19 October 2026** (section 9), on the owner's decision of 13 October 2026, with repository-local configuration (`git lfs install --local`, or `Scripts/setup-repo.sh` in a new clone) in place of the global `git lfs install`.
- **Owner:** decide the plan amendments proposed in draft 0.5 (`M0-plan-amendments.md`, which includes rows 3, 19 and 20) and review the `CLAUDE.md` changes (rows 22 and 30, and M0 assembly's in section 7.3). **Decided on 22 October 2026** (section 15): the amendments approved, as plan draft 0.8 records, and the `CLAUDE.md` changes that decision D-7 listed approved as written; the milestone paragraph rewritten after the decision page is not among them (`M0-gate.md` Section 7, item 5). (This note was added after the review of the decisions round, its finding CHK-06.)
- **G0.2:** `Spikes/G02-Transfer/MacIdentityProbe` had no committed lock, and swift-atomics, swift-collections and swift-system reached it only through swift-nio's version ranges (ADR-0014 open item); `Spikes/G02-Transfer/iOS` had none either. **Closed at M0 assembly:** G0.2 committed both locks as copies of `SourceService`'s and `TransferKit`'s on `m0-int-minors-g02-transfer`, the inventory moved them from `unlocked` to `locks`, and `12-dependency-lock` and the G0.2 drop-in's package-locks step check them (section 7).
- **G0.4 merge:** done on 14 October 2026 by the second integration (ADR-0006 and ADR-0007 linked, report and checklist linked, fixtures in `Fixtures/LICENSES.md`, no package for ADR-0014, section 2 row, evidence images under the LFS evidence pattern; sections 2 to 5).
- **G0.4:** review 6's open minor findings stay with the gate (their IDs were not given to the second integration); the device run (`device-checklists/G04-Audio.md`); the product-owner decisions in ADR-0006 (release codec list, per-file timing mode, import remux, mono excerpts) and ADR-0007 (quick-capture narrowing). **The decisions were taken on 22 October 2026** (section 15: ADR-0006's PO-1 to PO-5 as the amendments' PO-18, PO-20, PO-19 and PO-39, PO-39 otherwise than recommended, and ADR-0007's PO-6 as PO-17 (a)); the release codec list itself still follows the device run. (This note was added after the review of the decisions round, its finding CHK-06.)
- **`SylviaDesign` owner:** vocabulary keys for "nothing to capture", "the moment was not saved" and the player's `preparing` state, with design review (row 27).
- **Contract consolidation:** done on `m0-int-contracts` (ADR-0016), merged at M0 assembly, G0.4's audio anchor and excerpt fields included (row 28). Re-run `Docs/Stage0/evidence/M0-contracts/commands/check-g04-head.sh` against the merged commit (ADR-0016 open item): section 7.
- **Done on `m0-int-deps`:** ADR-0014 rewritten with the full inventory (`Docs/Licenses/`), the CI check `12-dependency-lock`, the advisory search and the acknowledgements draft; its pending-human items are in ADR-0014 section 7. Merged at M0 assembly with its final review's verdict "reject" and two serious findings open (section 7).
- **ADR-0015 threat model:** written on `m0-int-threat` (Proposed; security sign-off pending). Its CI drop-in `13-hard-rules` failed on TM-F1 until the G0.5 recovery `FileProtection.swift` fix, which M0 assembly applied together with TM-F2 (section 7); it passes since.
- **Whoever merges `m0-int-deps`:** that branch rewrites ADR-0014, whose status line and G0.4 open item the G0.4 merge also changed; both versions say G0.4 adds no package, so the conflict is textual only. Resolved at M0 assembly (section 7.1).
- **M0 integration (not done here):** the M0 gate summary `M0-gate.md` (ADR-0015 and the contract consolidation were done on their own branches and merged at M0 assembly).

## 7. M0 assembly (14-15 October 2026)

After the eight gates, thirteen M0 items were done on their own branches from `m0-integrate` (ten from `b354dc7`, the seven-gate merge; three from `471b143`, after the G0.4 merge), each finished with an agent review. The assembly squash-merged them into `m0-integrate` (from `471b1437e64aca8d5ae956db84cc0964657ccea8`), applied or recorded their shared-file requests, fixed what the full-CI verification of the seven-gate merge had found, and ran the complete CI from a clean tree. An assembly agent started this work and was stopped by a network outage after the thirteen merges and three work-in-progress commits; a second assembly agent re-checked that work as unreviewed (the merges below; the three commits in 7.2, of which one did not build, the TM-F2 change, found by the first complete run and corrected in `038145a`, and one was incomplete, the lock-flag change, completed in `4a44739`) and finished it. Every outcome is agent-reviewed, human sign-off pending. Host: macOS 26.6.2 (25G83), Apple M5 Max, Xcode 27.0 (27A266a), XcodeGen 2.46.0.

### 7.1 Item merges

No item was skipped: none had failed. **The deps item was merged with its final review's verdict "reject".** The task named it for merging and it had not failed, so it was merged and its two open serious findings are recorded here, in ADR-0014's status and in the ADR index rather than hidden. Both are routes around the CI guard that keeps the vulnerable marmelroy/Zip 2.1.2 (CVE-2023-39135) out of product code: **R4-01**, XcodeGen expands `${VAR}` from the environment, so a product `project.yml` can reach `Spikes/` code, add `-import-module Zip` or use a test-only package without the check seeing it; **R4-02**, C-family `#include` paths are judged only against `Spikes/`, so an include outside the product roots or into a package checkout (Minizip's headers included) passes. No product target exists yet, so no route is in use. They stay with ADR-0014's owner and must be closed before the first product target is added (open items below). *(Dated note, 20 October 2026, M0 final review round 2, evidence R2-05: "no product target exists yet" was wrong when written, since the design package's targets `Apps/DesignCatalog` and `Packages/Design` are product targets, and that deadline had already passed. Neither target links Readium or anything else that brings Zip, so neither route is in use. Since the M0 final fix (SEC-04) the deadline is: before the M1 app target is created, or before any product target links Readium if that comes first, in ADR-0014, ADR-0015, `M0-gate.md` and plan 23.2.)*

**One conflict, in wording only.** `m0-int-deps` (from `b354dc7`) rewrites ADR-0014, whose status line and G0.4 open item the G0.4 merge (`269accf`) had also changed. The branch's rewrite was kept; its status line adds that G0.4 was merged on 14 October 2026 without a package, and its "G0.4 merge" open item records that merge as done. Both sides said G0.4 adds no package.

### 7.2 Changes made at assembly beyond the merges

Code and scripts (each verified by the complete CI run in 7.6):

| Commit | Change | Why |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `04a948e` | TM-F1: `FileProtector.writingOptions` (G0.5 recovery) throws `unsupportedClass` for any class other than A, B and C before anything is written, and `write` removes a file whose class fails readback; test `DataProtectionTests.unsupportedClassRefused`. TM-F2: G0.4's `DurableFile` maps A, B and C to `URLFileProtection` (a `FileProtectionType` given to the resource key is accepted and ignored), refuses any other class before creating anything, and reads the class back; tests `DurableFileProtectionTests`. This first version of TM-F2 did not build (corrected in `038145a`, below) | ADR-0015 asked the G0.5 recovery and G0.4 owners for these fixes; both owners' branches are merged, so the assembly applied them. `13-hard-rules` failed on TM-F1 until then |
| `37b00c3` | `40-g02-transfer.sh` writes its results under `G02_EVIDENCE_DIR`, by default `.build/ci-results/g02-transfer` (the SourceService test harness and the background run read the same variable), and `G02_LOOPBACK_ONLY=1` lists the LAN, Tailscale and device-ATS-recipe steps as not run and gives the simulator runs no LAN or tailnet address, so no test server binds beyond loopback | The full-CI verification of the seven-gate merge (`b354dc7`) found R1-01 (the drop-in rewrote 90 committed G0.2 evidence files on every run) and R1-02 (a complete run started G0.2 test servers on the LAN and Tailscale addresses, beyond the agents' loopback-only rule) |
| `6d7b5f0` | `--force-resolved-versions` on the SwiftPM calls of the drop-ins and spike scripts ADR-0014 lists; `.github/workflows/ci.yml` pins `actions/checkout` and `actions/upload-artifact` to their release commits and fails unless XcodeGen reports 2.46.0, with the inventory's `ci_actions` updated; the inventory moves G0.2's two new locks from `unlocked` to `locks`; `TokenExport` status text and `Marker.captureKinds` comment, with `marginalia.v1.json` and the 14 design-token fixtures that embed the export regenerated; the contract fixture generator and mutation pass moved to `Scripts/contracts/` with the `11-capture-anchor-contracts` `tools=` line, ADR-0016 section 10 and `M0-contracts.md` updated; `fetch.sh` downloads to a temporary file of its own and renames it after the SHA-256 check | Requests of the deps, contracts and G0.3 items (7.3) |
| `4a44739` | `--force-resolved-versions` also on the SwiftPM calls added after ADR-0014's count (G0.3's `check-differential-sensitivity.sh`, `check-generator-overlap.sh`, `check-round4-before-fix.sh`, `host-pack-writer-scaling.sh`; G0.5 AI's `mutation-checks.py`); `Scripts/ci.sh` prints a passing drop-in's `WARN` lines | ADR-0014 decision 4 (every call); review R3-05 of the deps item. Not changed: the evidence commands under `Docs/Stage0/evidence/*/commands/`, which record how committed evidence was made, and G0.3's frozen copy of its round-2 check |
| `038145a` | **Fixes for the two failures of the first complete run (7.6).** G0.4: `DurableFile.applyProtection` sets the class through `NSURL.setResourceValue` with the `URLFileProtection` value, because `URLResourceValues.fileProtection`, which `04a948e` assigned, is get-only, so G0.4 did not build. A host check builds `DurableFile.swift` and reads back each of classes A, B and C as requested, and an unmapped class is refused before any directory is created (`evidence/M0-Integration/durable-file-protection-probe.log`, `commands/durable-file-protection-probe.sh`; *dated note, 19 October 2026, M0 final review R1-09: that log does not record the screen's lock state, on which classes A and B depend; the probe now records it and gives each class its own directory: `evidence/M0-Integration/final-fix/durable-file-protection-probe-unlocked.log`, PASS, and `evidence/M0-Integration/final-fix/durable-file-protection-probe-locked.log`, INCONCLUSIVE, section 11*); the G0.4 drop-in in its `G04_SKIP_REALAV=1` mode then built the signed device and simulator products and passed its 120 unit tests, `DurableFileProtectionTests` included (a pre-check, not a gate run; the second complete run is the evidence). G0.2: under `G02_LOOPBACK_ONLY=1` the background run leaves out `bonjourBrowseFindsResolvesAndPinsTheSource()` and lists it as not run (`simulator-bonjour-browse`), as it does the LAN steps; the test is unchanged and runs whenever the LAN is allowed | The first complete run (`4a44739`) failed in `60-g04-audio` (the build) and `40-g02-transfer` (the Bonjour browse found nothing in 20 s). Both were caused by assembly changes, `04a948e` and `37b00c3`. A loopback-only source registers its Bonjour service local to the Mac (`BonjourAdvertiser`, `kDNSServiceInterfaceIndexLocalOnly`), which the simulator's browser does not see: the test alone failed the same way at a load of 3.6 (17:19 UTC), so load is not the cause. With the change, `Scripts/ci.sh g02-transfer` passed (7 of the background run's 8 tests, 371 s). *(Dated note, 20 October 2026, M0 final review round 2, evidence R2-04: the run of the test alone at a load of 3.6 and the passing 371 s run are unlogged observations: no committed log has either. The committed evidence of the fix is the second complete run, 7.6.)* |
| `038145a` | `Scripts/validate-contracts.py`'s `load_json` also catches `ValueError` (a `UnicodeDecodeError`, or an integer longer than Python's conversion limit), so such a file is an error line instead of a traceback; `JSONDecodeError` keeps its message | G0.3 request (c). On a scratch copy of `Contracts/` with a Latin-1 fixture and a 5,000-digit integer, the validator prints one error line for each and exits 1, with no traceback, and the unchanged copy passes (`evidence/M0-Integration/validator-load-errors.log`, `commands/validator-load-errors.sh`) |

On the `--show-bin-path` calls: the relayed deps request said not to add the flag there, but ADR-0014's final text (open items) asks for it on those calls too, saying the flag costs nothing; the assembly followed the ADR, the item's final word.

`fetch.sh`'s new path was exercised with two concurrent fetches of the same missing file into an empty scratch cache: both succeeded, the file has its pinned SHA-256 and mode 644, and no temporary file remained (`evidence/M0-Integration/fetch-race.log`, `commands/fetch-race.sh`; one 6.4 MB public-domain download).

### 7.5 G0.4 capture probe against the merged commit

The contracts item asked for its G0.4 capture probe to be re-run against the merged commit (ADR-0016 open item), since the committed records come from G0.4 at `943d5e7` and G0.4 changed afterwards (round 6, and TM-F2 in `DurableFile`, which the capture store writes through). `Docs/Stage0/evidence/M0-contracts/commands/check-g04-head.sh 943d5e778de2b58f47343f0e146f9e3ed7baa70e 038145a` compiled G0.4's capture code at each commit with the probe (Swift 6, complete strict concurrency, warnings as errors: 11 files at `943d5e7`, 13 at `038145a`, 0 warnings) and ran it on the four-file LibriVox MP3 book. At `038145a` the members are in G0.4's estimated-timing mode for constant-bit-rate MP3; the probe made 5 records with the committed records' idempotency keys and the same format (every JSON key path the same), 2 excerpt files and 8 durable writes, the same exported ranges, frame counts and byte counts as at `943d5e7`, and `Scripts/check-spike-anchors.py` passed with these records in place of the committed ones (0 problems). Nothing was played: the probe decodes and encodes only. The script writes its log over the committed `M0-contracts/g04-head-check.log`; the assembly's run is kept as `evidence/M0-Integration/g04-head-check-assembled.log`, and the committed log was restored unchanged. The optional G0.4 test that would make the probe unnecessary (row 39) is for M4.

Both complete runs used `Docs/Stage0/evidence/M0-Integration/commands/ci-assembled.sh` (an identical copy under `.build/scratch/` ran them): `Scripts/ci.sh` from a clean tree (no `.build/DerivedData` anywhere, no CI outputs), after `Scripts/fixtures/fetch.sh` and the eight cache generators the drop-ins need (`commands/prep-fixtures.sh`; every download and generated file was present and verified, `prep-fixtures-assembled.log`). Every drop-in used this agent's one simulator name, `Sylvia-M0-assemble` (iPhone 17, iOS 26.5; the design catalog also made `-SE` and `-iPad` simulators of that name, and G0.1 PDF one on iOS 27.0; all deleted afterwards), with `G02_LOOPBACK_ONLY=1`, `SYLVIA_AUDIBLE_PLAYBACK` unset (every player at test volume, 1 %, so every audio measurement was taken at test volume) and no evidence-folder variable, so every drop-in wrote under `.build/`. `G04_DEVICE_FIXTURES` was not set: the 8.6 GB device bundle with the fixtures was not rebuilt (the signed generic-device build without them was). Up to 13 other agents shared the Mac: the load is recorded at the start and end of each run, in each drop-in's log and in G0.4's 30-second samples, and every timing below is indicative only.

**First run, on `4a44739` (14 October 2026, 14:40-16:40 UTC): failed in two drop-ins, both caused by assembly changes** (`ci-full-clean-run-assembled-first.log`; the failing drop-ins' logs `ci-first-run-60-g04-audio.log`, `ci-first-run-40-g02-transfer.log` and the failing test lines `ci-first-run-g02-background-tests.log`). Passed: `10-contracts` (17 schemas, 644 fixtures), `11-capture-anchor-contracts`, `12-dependency-lock`, `13-hard-rules` (TM-F1 no longer reported), `20-design`, `21-design-catalog` (2,640 s), `31-g01-epub`, `32-g01-pdf`, `50-g03-intake` (1,897 s), `70-g05-ai`, `71-g05-recovery`. Failed: `60-g04-audio`, whose build stopped on `04a948e`'s assignment to the get-only `URLResourceValues.fileProtection`; and `40-g02-transfer`, whose background run's `bonjourBrowseFindsResolvesAndPinsTheSource()` found no Bonjour result in 20 s, because `37b00c3`'s loopback-only sources register their service local to the Mac. The 1-minute load reached 83 during the G0.2 drop-in; the Bonjour test failed the same way alone at a load of 3.6, so load was not the cause. *(Dated note, 19 October 2026, M0 final review R1-03: no committed log records that load of 83 or the run of the test alone; `ci-first-run-40-g02-transfer.log` and `ci-full-clean-run-assembled-first.log` record loads of 3.16-7.49. Both statements are the assembly agent's observations, not logged.)* Both were fixed in `038145a` (7.2). After the run no tracked file had changed (`git status` showed only this agent's own uncommitted edits): no drop-in wrote over committed evidence (R1-01).

**Second run, on `efc9479` (the fixes of `038145a` plus documents; 14 October 2026, 17:31-20:53 UTC, from a clean tree: every `.build` directory deleted, this agent's simulators deleted and recreated by the drop-ins, and the fixtures checked again): 12 of 13 drop-ins passed, and the thirteenth failed only on its locked-screen guard** (`ci-full-clean-run-assembled.log`). *(Dated note, 19 October 2026, M0 final review R1-03: `ci-assembled.sh` logs no deletion, and `prep-fixtures-assembled.log` is the first run's (from 14:39 UTC), so for this run the deletion and the fixture check are unlogged observations; so are the G0.2 not-run list and its server addresses, the G0.4 test count, its known issues and its volume lines below, because only `ci.sh`'s pass lines and G0.4's load samples were kept from this run. After the run no tracked file had changed (only this agent's own edit to this log): no drop-in wrote over committed evidence (R1-01). ## 9. Git LFS adoption (19 October 2026)

The product owner decided on 13 October 2026: "Adopt Git LFS for the snapshot and evidence images once the current integration is complete. Use repository-local configuration, update CI and setup instructions, and verify a clean checkout. Leave existing history intact." An agent carried it out on `m0-integrate`, starting from `504fac2e0c37efa5b41ed4503752758b103bc00d` (the branch after the merge of `main`'s README changes, pull request #4); the attempt recorded in section 8 had stopped before making a commit. Agent review and human sign-off pending. Host: macOS 26.6.2 (25G83), Apple M5 Max, git 2.55.0 and git-lfs 3.7.1 from Homebrew. Every log below is in `evidence/M0-Integration/lfs/` and was written by the command named beside it (in that folder's `commands/`); ADR-0002 records the decision, the patterns, the configuration, the CI and setup changes and the push-time cost.

### 9.1 Commits

`git log 504fac2..m0-integrate` lists them. The adoption itself is one commit, `adab8e2a3b69671ddabe7963793f41a828620ffb`, "Track snapshot and evidence images with Git LFS", whose only parent is `504fac2`: it adds `.gitattributes` and turns exactly the 777 matched images into LFS pointers with `git add --renormalize`. The commits after it add the step 1-5 evidence (`ccc64c5`), the CI check (`5fdc7ef`: `Scripts/ci.d/00-git-lfs.sh`, `Scripts/ci.sh` running the `00-*` drop-ins first in every run, and `lfs: true` in the workflow), `Scripts/setup-repo.sh` (`8c2adbc`), the evidence commands (`28d7501`, `3cefd30`, `56cb0ef`), one-line self-test output in CI runs (`3021ddd`) and an exact pass line (`77d6cf7`) for `00-git-lfs`, the documents (`CLAUDE.md` Setup, binary files and CI; ADR-0002; this section; `README.md`; the git-lfs rows of `Docs/Licenses/inventory.json` and `third-party-inventory.md`) and the step 6-8 logs. No history was rewritten: no `git lfs migrate`, filter-branch, rebase or amend. The fixes for the verification's findings follow as new commits (section 9.6).

**How the branch reached the base** (a gap the verification noted; `commands/base-reflog.sh`, `r3-fix/base-reflog.log`). The reflog shows that at 05:45:37 NZDT on 19 October 2026 a `fetch -q origin` fast-forwarded `origin/main` to `504fac2`, and that at 05:45:59 `m0-integrate` was moved from `177ed9a`, the commit pushed on 15 October 2026, to `504fac2` ("branch: Reset to 504fac2"). Both happened before the adoption agent's worktree was created (05:46:51); the reflog does not say who ran them. `177ed9a` is `504fac2`'s second parent, so the move only added the merge of pull request #4 and lost no commit. `history-before.log` (step 2) was taken after it. The adoption's instructions allowed no fetch from `origin`; this one came before the adoption began.

### 9.4 Step 8: clean checkouts

`commands/clean-checkout.sh /Users/jevidon/Git/sylvia <scratch-dir> <phase>`, through `commands/run-logged.sh`. The full design run, `20-design` and `21-design-catalog` (47 minutes), ran once, earlier, in a clone of `28d7501`; between that commit and `77d6cf7` the only changes outside `Docs/` are a comment in the workflow, `CLAUDE.md`, and the output of `00-git-lfs` (one line per self-test case in CI runs, and its pass line), none of which the design drop-ins read (`design-run-to-docs-commit-diff.txt`). The first lines of that run's two `run-logged.sh` logs (`clean-checkout-design-clone-setup.log`, `clean-checkout-design.log`) name this worktree's commit at the time (`28d7501`, then `3cefd30`) and one or two evidence commands not yet committed; the clone's own commit, `28d7501`, is on their `HEAD:` lines. Every clone was made with `git clone --no-local` from the local repository (its `origin` is that path), so nothing came from the network. git-lfs needed no setting for the local-path remote: in each clone, `git lfs env` shows the endpoint `file:///Users/jevidon/Git/sylvia/.git` and the built-in `lfs-standalone-file` transfer, which copies the objects from the repository's own LFS storage into the clone's. Each clone first checks out the local repository's current branch (at the time `codex/readme-platform-vision`, which has no `.gitattributes`) and then `m0-integrate`.

- **Pushing is the owner's.** Nothing was pushed or fetched by the adoption or its fixes, and GitHub was not contacted (the fetch that came before the adoption began is in section 9.1). The first push of `m0-integrate` uploads the 719 LFS objects through the `pre-push` hook, against the owner's GitHub LFS storage (ADR-0002, Push-time cost, with GitHub's documented quotas, read on 19 October 2026). The GitHub workflow, now with `lfs: true`, has not been run (manual dispatch only).
- **Xcode on the build Mac is 27.1, not the pinned 27.0** (not part of this change). `/Applications/Xcode.app` is Xcode 27.1 (27A9275), modified on 18 October 2026 at 07:28 NZDT; ADR-0001 pins 27.0 (27A266a), and no Xcode 27.0 is installed. So `12-dependency-lock` fails its toolchain check on this Mac whatever the commit (section 9.3), and the design drop-ins of section 9.4 were built with Xcode 27.1 (they warn and continue). Owner: reinstall Xcode 27.0, or update ADR-0001, ADR-0014 and the inventory's toolchain pins. *(Dated note, 20 October 2026: PO-35 was decided for 27.1, as ADR-0001 records the reading of the product owner's reply, and the pins changed together: section 13.)* The first `xcrun simctl` call of this session under Xcode 27.1 printed that it installed that Xcode's simulator components and replaced the running CoreSimulator service (framework 1174.9.2, previous service 1171.7); the device list taken right after showed no booted simulator. That was seen in the session's output and is not kept as a log.

### 9.6 Verification findings R3-01 to R3-03, fixed (19 October 2026)

The adoption's verification (an agent review; human sign-off pending) left three minor findings. They were fixed with new commits on `m0-integrate`, `75b312f` to `9ac7789`: nothing was amended or rewritten, no `git lfs migrate` was run, and no LFS pattern and no LFS file changed. The checks below then ran again on `9ac7789`. The commits after it change only files under `Docs/`: the logs, this section, and in `672d252` a clearer wording of runbook D-8 and the header comment of `commands/doc-checks-before-after.sh`. The logs are in `evidence/M0-Integration/lfs/r3-fix/`, except `lfs-storage.log`, which the corrected command rewrote in place. Each log's first lines name its commit, the number of uncommitted files in this worktree at its start (none, except where a row says otherwise) and the load at its start and end. Host as above, with Xcode 27.1 (27A9275; section 9.5).

- **R3-01, the shared-object count** (`57094b1`). `commands/lfs_storage.py` printed 777 files less 719 objects, 58, with the words "files share an object with another file", but 108 files share 50 objects. It now prints how the files share the objects and stops if the counts do not add up: 669 objects are each used by one file, and 50 are each shared by several files (44 by 2 files, 4 by 3, 2 by 4), 108 files in all, so there are 58 fewer objects than files. It counts from each file's SHA-256 in the inventory, which is the manifest's and so the file's pointer oid (`r3-fix/verify-adoption-final.log`, check 3). Run on `9ac7789`, it rewrote `lfs-storage.log`: 719 objects in the repository's LFS storage, each with its size and SHA-256. The totals were right and are unchanged: 777 files, 719 objects, 65,110,465 bytes. Section 9.2's row now gives the corrected statement.
- **R3-02, documents that still described LFS as a future step** (`13254bc`; D-8's wording made unambiguous in `672d252`). Runbook D-8 told the owner to run `git lfs install` on each Mac, which without `--local` writes the global git configuration, and then to adopt LFS. D-8 now says that the decision is taken and the adoption done; that every clone is set up with `Scripts/setup-repo.sh`, never with `git lfs install` without `--local`; and what the adoption leaves the owner: the first push, which uploads the LFS objects; the GitHub workflow, not yet run with Git LFS; and MP4 evidence, which the image patterns do not cover. Runbook step 0.3 says to run `Scripts/setup-repo.sh` in a clone not yet set up for Git LFS. `G06-Design.md` open question 8 and the two `M0-plan-amendments.md` entries that pointed to ADR-0002's old trigger (its Section 3.1 row and the repository owner's item in Section 5) keep their text and add a dated note of the decision and the adoption. Two consistency checks read some of these documents: the amendments' `check-amendments.py` (`M0-plan-amendments.md` and the reports, `G06-Design.md` among them) and the runbook's `check-correction-texts.py` (the files whose texts the runbook's corrections replace, `G06-Design.md` among them); neither reads the runbook's own text, and no committed check does. Both give the same output on the trees before and after the edits (`commands/doc-checks-before-after.sh 57094b1 672d252`, `doc-checks-before-after.log`). Both fail already before the edits, and the edits change nothing in either output: 14 of the amendments checker's checks fail, and 59 of the 77 texts that the runbook's corrections quote are not found. Those failures are outside this change; for the amendments, `M0-gate.md` Section 6.3 item 5 already records that their re-check against the merged reports is not recorded. `M0-gate.md` (Section 5.3, its D-8 row and R2-01) is left to the gate summary refresh.
- **R3-03, the `.gitattributes` comment** (`75b312f`). The comment said a file matches the same way on every platform whatever `core.ignorecase` says. That holds for the extensions, which are letter-case classes, not for the directory names. The comment now says that git matches `__Snapshots__` and `Docs/Stage0/evidence` in another letter case only where `core.ignorecase` is true, so they are to be kept exactly as written. Only the comment changed: `commands/check_attr_proof.py` over all 2,975 paths tracked at `9ac7789`, with that commit's `.gitattributes`, gives `filter=lfs diff=lfs merge=lfs -text` to exactly the 777 manifest paths and to nothing else, with either `core.ignorecase` setting (`check-attr.log`). Because both settings select the same paths, no tracked image lies in a directory whose name differs from the patterns' only in letter case. `commands/case-probe.sh` (`case-probe.log`) checked the new wording in a temporary repository with this `.gitattributes` and six stand-in PNG files committed as plain blobs, three of them in case-variant directories (`docs/stage0/evidence/`, `Docs/Stage0/Evidence/`, `__snapshots__/`), put into the index directly because this Mac's file system cannot hold `docs/` beside `Docs/`. `git check-attr` gives the three `filter=lfs` with `core.ignorecase=true` and not with `false`. In the same repository git-lfs 3.7.1's `git lfs fsck --pointers`, which `00-git-lfs`'s committed-blob check runs, reported all six as blobs that should have been pointers, the three case variants included, with either setting. The verification had seen it miss two such paths; this probe does not reproduce that.

## 10. The M0 work on `main`, and the gate summary refresh (19 October 2026)

By an agent on `m0-integrate`, on top of `e3e8886`; agent-written, human sign-off pending. Host: macOS 26.6.2 (25G83), Apple M5 Max, Xcode 27.1 (27A9275; section 9.5), git-lfs 3.7.1. No simulator was created or used, nothing played audio, and nothing was fetched or pushed.

### 10.1 The merge into `main`

On 15 October 2026 the product owner merged `m0-integrate` into `main` through GitHub pull request #4. The merge commit `504fac2` has the parents `4c49b5b` (`main`) and `177ed9a` (`m0-integrate`, which had just merged `main`'s 15 October changes: plan draft 0.6, the Companion Vision addendum's sequencing note and the README), and its tree is exactly `177ed9a`'s. The merge came before the M0 gate was approved. It changes no approval status, and the gate stays pending. `m0-integrate` was then fast-forwarded to `504fac2` (section 9.1 records the reflog), and the later M0 work, the Git LFS adoption and this refresh, continues on it and goes to `main` by a follow-up pull request. All of this is read from the local repository, which last fetched `origin/main` at `504fac2`; nothing was fetched here (`gate-refresh-checks.log`, part 2).

Statements that described the M0 work as separate or unmerged were updated as statements of fact. The pinned links to `d8e7d39` are kept: they resolve, because that commit is in this branch's history.

The front matter's Revised line and a dated bullet in Appendix D's draft 0.6 entry record the edit, which changes no decision, amendment or gate status |

The documents were searched with `git grep` for "separately reviewed", "unmerged", "not merged", "is integrated", "maintained separately", "remain separate", "once it is merged" and similar wording. The other hits describe a branch's state when a record was written (ADR-0015 and ADR-0016 on G0.4, `M0-contracts.md`, the third-party inventory's G0.4 section, the amendments' "Sources changing on unmerged branches", Appendix D's entries, ADR-0002's kept deferral) and were left as history. `CLAUDE.md` was not changed: it says the work is combined on `m0-integrate`, which is still true, and changes to it wait for the owner's review.

Rows 22, 30 and 83 asked the owner to review the `CLAUDE.md` changes before `m0-integrate` is merged. The merge came first and no review is recorded, so the request stands (a dated note in rows 22 and 83). *(Dated note, 23 October 2026: the owner approved those changes as written on 22 October 2026, decision D-7, section 15; added after the review of the decisions round's fix, its finding FID2-01.)* ADR-0002's branch rule says a milestone's branch is merged after its exit review, and this merge came before it. Both are in `M0-gate.md` Section 6.3, for the owner.

## 11. M0 final fix (19 October 2026)

By an agent on `m0-integrate`, on top of `fea12c3`, the commit the M0 final review read; agent-written, not reviewed again, human sign-off pending. Host: macOS 26.6.2 (25G83), Apple M5 Max, Xcode 27.1 (27A9275), not the pinned 27.0 (section 9.5), git-lfs 3.7.1. Simulators: this agent's own `Sylvia-M0-final-fix` (iPhone 17, iOS 26.5) and those the drop-ins make from its name (the design catalog's `-SE` and `-iPad`, and G0.1 PDF's iOS 27.0 one), each deleted when the fix ends; every player at test volume 0.01, so no measurement here was taken at full volume. Network use: pinned Swift package resolution and `Scripts/fixtures/fetch.sh` (every fixture was already in the shared cache and was only verified); nothing was pushed, and nothing fetched from a git remote.

### 11.1 The findings, and what was done

The review handed over 32 findings from five lenses: evidence R1-01 to R1-09, clean clone CC-01 to CC-05, contracts R1-01 to R1-08, security SEC-01 to SEC-08, and plan R1-01 and R1-02. Its list was cut off after the title of plan R1-02; whatever came after it was not received, so it is not addressed here. *(Dated note, 20 October 2026: the second review round relayed two more of this review's findings, plan R1-03 and R1-04 (minor), and its owner-readability findings name R5-01 to R5-04 of this review, three of them major, as unfixed: they were in the part not received; so the review raised at least 38 findings, not 32. Section 12 records what was done with each.)* Finding numbers repeat across lenses, so each is named with its lens. Evidence paths are relative to `Docs/Stage0/evidence/`.

### 11.3 The complete CI run from a fresh clone

`evidence/M0-Integration/final-fix/commands/ci-final-clone.sh` made a new clone of this repository with `git clone --no-local` at `.build/scratch/final-clone` (a path holding `/.build/`, the case of CC-01; nothing came from the network: the LFS objects were copied from the repository's own LFS storage), checked out `e3c8659`, the fix's code, with no uncommitted file, set up Git LFS in the clone's configuration with `Scripts/setup-repo.sh` (777 files, none a pointer), checked the fixtures with M0 assembly's `commands/prep-fixtures.sh`, and ran `Scripts/ci.sh` there, so no build product, DerivedData or CI output of any earlier run existed. Every drop-in used `Sylvia-M0-final-fix`, `G02_LOOPBACK_ONLY=1` and test volume, and no evidence-folder variable, so every drop-in wrote under the clone's `.build/` and no committed evidence was touched (`git status` in the clone after the run: 0 lines). Host: Xcode 27.1 (27A9275). Run: 03:10-06:21 UTC on 19 October 2026 (`evidence/M0-Integration/final-fix/ci-full-clean-run-final.log`, moved there on 20 October 2026 when the second round's complete run took its old name); every drop-in's own log is kept in `evidence/M0-Integration/final-fix/ci-logs/` (with the hard-rule audit's two logs and G0.3's host and simulator test logs), and a sampler recorded the one-minute load and the booted simulators every minute (`evidence/M0-Integration/final-fix/ci-full-clean-run-final-load.tsv`: load 1.1-40.6 over 191 samples; never more than one simulator booted, this job's, and none at the samples between drop-ins).

The drop-in then stopped, before its process-kill, portability and quick-capture steps |
| `70-g05-ai` | pass | 93 s | |
| `71-g05-recovery` | FAIL | 33 s | Built from clean; its 73 host tests passed, among them the locked-host enforcement test, which runs only while the screen is locked; then its row-10 guard stopped it, because the Mac's screen had locked and the class-B tests skip while it is locked ("class A/B keys are unavailable (host screen locked)"); its simulator tests were not reached |

**Why `60-g04-audio` failed.** The Mac's default output device was AirPlay for the whole drop-in (*dated note, 20 October 2026, M0 final review round 2, evidence R2-03: from 05:10 UTC, when that record starts; the drop-in's tests started at 05:03 UTC, `final-fix/ci-logs/60-g04-audio.log`, so its first seven minutes have no record of the device*) (`evidence/M0-Integration/final-fix/audio-device-state.log`, `system_profiler` every five minutes from 05:10 UTC, read-only: agents never change the Mac's audio device, volume or mute state; the built-in speakers were the system output). The iOS Simulator plays through the default output device, and an AVPlayer's clock includes that device's output latency. Every failure has the same signature, the player's clock about 2.3 s behind the audio the engine rendered: 0.53-0.59 s of media per second at 1x over seconds 1 to 4 of playback (that is, the clock started about 2.3 s late), and the same at 2x and 3x; a preview overrun of 2.37-2.42 s past its end; a lead of 6.7-6.8 s of media at 3x between the taps and the clock in the soak; Now Playing's elapsed time still 0 when read; and a resume that looked for the first audio within 0.5 s of the clock's position and found none. Meanwhile the taps received the audio at the normal rate (33 chunks in 3 s at 1x, as in the gate's committed `audio-output.txt`) and pitch (1,000 Hz). Each failed test reads the player's clock; those that measure decoded or rendered audio alone, such as the codec matrix, the excerpt timing and the pending previews' boundaries in every format, passed. The G0.4 code and drop-in are unchanged since `efc9479`, where the drop-in passed (the output device was not recorded then), and the final review's own run passed it under Xcode 27.1 (CC-03; that run's logs are not committed, so this is an unlogged report, not evidence: no committed log shows the drop-in passing under 27.1). The seven tests were re-run in the same clone with the run's build products (`evidence/M0-Integration/final-fix/g04-rerun-failed.log`, 06:22-07:06 UTC, the output device recorded at its start and end): AirPlay was still the default output, and the same seven failed with the same signature (for example 0.53 and 0.59 s of media per second at 1x in the quiet-volume check). So the failures are reproducible on this Mac as it is set up now, and every measurement points at the output device's latency, not at the code; a run with the built-in output is still needed to show it (11.4). *(Dated note, 20 October 2026: that run was made in the second round, with the same code and Xcode, and every test passed, the player's clock advancing 1.000 s of media per second at 1x: section 12.4.)*

**`71-g05-recovery`.** The drop-in built from clean and passed its 73 host tests, the locked-host enforcement test among them (it runs only while the screen is locked), and then stopped at its row-10 guard, because the class-B tests skip while the screen is locked (`evidence/M0-Integration/final-fix/ci-logs/71-g05-recovery.log`); its simulator tests were not reached. `DataProtectionTests.unsupportedClassRefused`, the test SEC-06 extended, is one of the 73. A re-run with the screen unlocked is pending: an agent may not unlock the Mac, and the screen stayed locked from the end of the run until this fix ended (`evidence/M0-Integration/final-fix/lock-state.log`, read each minute with `ioreg`).

**After the run** nothing tracked had changed in the clone, every simulator of this job was shut down, and the run's drop-in logs were copied into `evidence/M0-Integration/final-fix/ci-logs/` (G0.4's through `commands/filter-g04-log.sh`, which leaves out the harness process's own system-log lines, 85 % of that log). The documents committed after `e3c8659` change no code: `evidence/M0-Integration/final-fix/gate-final-checks.log` checks that (part 1) and runs the drop-ins that read documents again on the last commit (part 5).

- **CC-03:** the owner decides PO-35 (reinstall Xcode 27.0, or change the pins together); no agent changes the pins. *(Dated note, 20 October 2026: decided by the owner for 27.1, and the pins changed on that decision: section 13.)*
- **CC-04:** the runner image: the owner chooses a runner with Xcode 27.0 and the iOS 26.5 runtime, or a self-hosted one. The workflow was not run on GitHub. *(Dated note, 20 October 2026: since the re-pin the runner needs Xcode 27.1 (27A9275), which the guard names: section 13.)*
- **Contracts R1-02:** G0.3's `created_at` is not tightened to UTC (recorded as an exception for M2), and the three UTC spellings are not unified (for M1, M5 and M6 to agree).
- **`60-g04-audio` with the Mac's built-in output:** the complete run and the re-run of its seven failed tests had AirPlay as the default output device (11.3); a run with the built-in output, which also reaches the drop-in's process-kill, portability and quick-capture steps, needs the owner, or an agent on a Mac whose default output is not AirPlay: agents never change the Mac's audio device. *(Dated note, 20 October 2026: done in the second round, when the Mac's default output had become the built-in speakers: the drop-in passed every step, the seven clock tests and the last three steps included, section 12.4.)*
- **`71-g05-recovery` with the screen unlocked:** its class-B readback of the final fix's code (SEC-06 changed the protector's directory and protect paths) and its simulator tests need a run with the screen unlocked, which an agent cannot arrange (11.3). *(Dated note, 20 October 2026, M0 final review round 2, evidence R2-02 and clean clone CC2-02: only the class-B readback (report row 10) needs the screen unlocked. The drop-in's own switch `G05_ALLOW_MISSING_CLASS_B=1` runs its other steps on a locked Mac, simulator tests included, and says such a run is not evidence for row 10. The second round ran the drop-in with that switch on the current code, and found the screen unlocked, so the class-B readback ran too: every step passed, row 10 included: section 12.)*
- **Device and human steps:** none ran; nothing here is a device result.
- **Agent review of this fix:** none yet; human sign-off pending.

## 12. M0 final fix, second round (20 October 2026)

By an agent on `m0-integrate`, on top of `83ee280`, the commit the M0 final review's second round read; agent-written, not reviewed again, human sign-off pending. Host: macOS 26.6.2 (25G83), Apple M5 Max, Xcode 27.1 (27A9275), not the pinned 27.0 (section 9.5), git-lfs 3.7.1. Simulators: this agent's own `Sylvia-M0-final-fix` (iPhone 17, iOS 26.5) and those the drop-ins make from its name (the design catalog's `-SE` and `-iPad`, and G0.1 PDF's iOS 27.0 one), each deleted when the round ends; every player at test volume 0.01, so no measurement here was taken at full volume. Network use: pinned Swift package resolution and `Scripts/fixtures/fetch.sh` (every fixture was already in the shared cache and was only verified); nothing was pushed, and nothing fetched from a git remote. Times are UTC and given with their UTC date; the build Mac's clock (NZDT, UTC+13) already read 20 October 2026, the date this section's title and the documents' dated notes use.

## 13. The toolchain re-pin, and the final review's third round (20 October 2026)

By an agent on `m0-integrate`, on top of `dc81fce`, the commit the M0 final review's third round read; agent-written, not reviewed, human sign-off pending. Host: macOS 26.6.2 (25G83), Apple M5 Max, Xcode 27.1 (27A9275), git-lfs 3.7.1. One simulator, `Sylvia-M0-repin-fix-1` (iPhone 17, iOS 26.5), created only to show that the iOS 26.5 runtime still boots under Xcode 27.1's simulator components, booted, shut down and deleted when the round ended; no test ran on it and nothing played audio. No build ran, so no package was resolved and no fixture fetched; nothing was pushed or fetched from a git remote, and no other network use. Times are UTC.

### 13.1 The decision of PO-35, and the re-pin

**The question.** Xcode on the build Mac updated itself from the Mac App Store to 27.1 (27A9275) on 18 October 2026 (section 9.5), and `12-dependency-lock` failed its toolchain check on every commit after it, the pin being 27.0 (27A266a). PO-35 (runbook D-3) asked the product owner to reinstall 27.0 and repeat the complete CI under it, or to pin 27.1, and recommended pinning 27.1, with the App Store's automatic updates turned off. *(Dated note, 20 October 2026, the review of the re-pin round, R8-03 and TC-01: that is the recommendation of the message the owner answered, as the workflow relayed it. The documents at `dc81fce` recommended (b) on a condition, "once the release notes show 27.1 is a release build" (`M0-gate.md` PO-35; runbook D-3 the same in other words), while the amendments still recommended (a) after a check of the release notes; and none of them mentioned the automatic updates. The condition was never met: section 14.)*

**The decision, and its basis.** The product owner's reply of 20 October 2026, relayed to this round by the workflow, was "don't know how to pin xcode", and the workflow gave this round the decision as the owner's: re-pin to the installed 27.1. The reply did not name an option. This round records it as option (b), the recommended one, which needs nothing done in Xcode: the pin is the repository's record of the build its checks expect. ADR-0001 records the decision with this basis, and that option (a) is still open if that is not what the owner meant. The workflow's relay also said the owner would turn off the App Store's automatic updates; the reply does not say so, so the documents ask it of the owner, with how to do it (ADR-0001; `M0-gate.md` Section 5.2; runbook D-3), and record that the updates were still on (`toolchain-state.log`: `AutoUpdate` 1, read-only).

**What changed together** (ADR-0014 section 6): `Scripts/lib/env.sh` (`SYLVIA_XCODE_BUILD_PINNED="27A9275"`); `Docs/Licenses/inventory.json` (the toolchain's Xcode, SDKs and evidence, with the pin's history, and the Xcode host-tool row) and `third-party-inventory.md`; `.github/workflows/ci.yml` (its comment and its guard, which looks for `/Applications/Xcode_27.1.app` or `/Applications/Xcode.app`, accepts only the exact line `Build version 27A9275`, and otherwise stops with an error naming the pinned build); `CLAUDE.md`'s toolchain line (a change for the owner's review, D-7); ADR-0001 (the decision, its basis, the evidence under 27.1, and that the gate evidence was produced on 27.0 and the Git LFS adoption, the final review and its fix on 27.1); ADR-0002's CI paragraph; ADR-0014 (status, sections 3 and 6, Evidence); plan 6.3 (A01) with a dated note in Appendix D and the front matter's revision date, and the amendments checker's anchor for that date; the comment of `Scripts/ci.d/50-g03-intake.sh` that named the Xcode it needs; the G0.6 checklist's install line; the runbook (1.1, step 0.2, Section 3's run 1, D-3); the gate summary; this log's dated notes in 9.5, 11.4 and 12.5. The drop-ins that call `sylvia_check_xcode` (20, 21, 31, 32, 40, 60 and 70) only warn on a mismatch, so their behaviour changes only in that they stop warning.

**What was checked** (`evidence/M0-Integration/repin-27.1/`, each log naming its command in `commands/`):

By an agent on `m0-integrate`, on top of `d66582e`, the re-pin round's last commit, which the review read; agent-written, not reviewed, human sign-off pending. Host: macOS 26.6.2 (25G83), Apple M5 Max, Xcode 27.1 (27A9275), git-lfs 3.7.1. No simulator was created or used: none of this fix's checks needs one, so the name it was given, `Sylvia-M0-repin-fix-2`, was never used, and nothing played audio. By an agent on branch `m0-dec-stage0`, made from `4c73da57` (`main` after pull request #5, which holds all the M0 work), as one of the workstreams that record the decisions in parallel. ### 15.3 The runbook content check, and the LFS files of this worktree

**Why a new version.** The content check of the re-pin round's fix, `evidence/M0-Integration/repin-27.1/round-2/commands/runbook-content-check.py`, fails on the runbook as the decisions leave it, and only because of them: correction C56 replaced the G0.3 checklist's Setup 1 text that one of its entries reads in its source; decision D-1 (b) replaced the plan 19.1 baseline the runbook quoted, and correction C50 the ADR-0013 open item it quoted; and G0.5 recovery step 3, which PO-23 (b) moves to the locked-device harness, is left to its checklist, so the runbook no longer carries that step's two log fields. `evidence/M0-Decisions/stage0/commands/runbook-content-check.py` replaces it with the same comparison and changed lists: G0.5 recovery step 3 joins the steps the runbook leaves to its checklist (with the runbook's words for it, which must stay in the runbook), and the three runbook-own texts the runbook no longer quotes leave its list (60 entries, 52 read in their source; 63 and 55 before). Its runner keeps every earlier control and limit, and adds two controls: the earlier version run on the runbook as committed must fail with exactly those six problems and no other, and the runbook without its words for step 3 must fail. The descriptions of the check in the runbook (header, Section 9), `M0-gate.md` 5.1 and the README say so.

**LFS files.** The workflow made this worktree with its 777 LFS files as pointer files, so `00-git-lfs` failed at first (the CI stopped there: `Scripts/ci.sh` runs no other drop-in after a failed checkout check). `git lfs fsck --objects` found every object in the local store, and `git lfs checkout` replaced the pointers from it, with nothing downloaded; the repository's configuration was not touched. Every run below had the files themselves.

**The shell.** The isolation guard of this session refuses `source` in the agent's shell, so `Scripts/lib/env.sh`, `sim.sh` and `xcodegen.sh` were not sourced at the prompt: every script run here sources `env.sh` itself, and the one direct `xcodebuild -version` set `DEVELOPER_DIR=/Applications/Xcode.app/Contents/Developer`. No simulator or XcodeGen was needed.

## 16. The lock harness of PO-23 (b), built in Stage 0 (22 October 2026)

By an agent on branch `m0-dec-harness`, made from `4c73da57`, one of the four workstreams that recorded the product owner's decisions of 22 October 2026 in parallel; this entry was asked for by that workstream and written at the assembly (section 17). Commits `79a3c407` to `1adb8f27`. Agent-written and agent-checked; no review yet; human sign-off pending. Nothing ran on a device.

- **What was built.** The product owner chose PO-23 (b): build the background-audio harness for the locked-device test T50 (G0.5 recovery device step 3) now, in Stage 0, before the device run, so that the run is conclusive the first time; the app-level unavailable state before the first unlock stays in M1 with the app shell. The harness is the app "G05 Lock", `Spikes/G05-Recovery/LockHarness`. While a run is active it plays a quiet tone (UIBackgroundModes audio, player volume 0.01, never muted; full volume only with `SYLVIA_AUDIBLE_PLAYBACK=1`) so that iOS keeps it running while the phone is locked. Every 2 s it saves a capture to the spike's class-C store (D17: WAL, `synchronous = FULL`, `fullfsync`), reads it back byte for byte, writes and reads a class-C file and reads every file's protection class, logging each attempt to a class-C log file and to the unified log; it records `isProtectedDataAvailable` and its notifications, a class-A canary that detects the lock, gaps (suspension), audio interruptions, restarts (from the boot time) and launches before the first unlock. It asks for no class but C, the canary aside. Its logic sits behind an injectable clock and protected-data source, and 43 simulator tests in 4 suites drive every lock state and failure on a simulated phone. *(Dated note, 22 October 2026: not every failure. A write of the run's record that failed was not counted as a failed attempt, and no test made one fail; nor did any check launch the app itself. The fix of the review of the decisions round added both, with 47 tests and a UI smoke test of the app: its findings H-02 and H-05; section 18.)*
- **Where it is wired.** `Scripts/ci.d/71-g05-recovery.sh` runs `Spikes/G05-Recovery/LockHarness/Scripts/harness-checks.sh` as its steps 13-15: the harness's `Package.resolved` against the recovery package's, the unit tests on the drop-in's simulator, and the app built and signed for a generic iOS device (build only). The device checklist's step 3 is rewritten around the harness (install, start, lock for 5 minutes, restart, unlock, record; the expected lines; the inconclusive and failed rules), and its header names device A, the iPhone 11 Pro. `G05-Recovery.md` gains rows 25 (passed-simulator: the logic), 26 (pending-device: the device run) and 27 (passed-host: the signed device build), and its open questions 1-6, F5 and F11 record the decisions that bear on them (PO-21, PO-22, PO-23, PO-40, D-1, D02 with D-6, D17, and the owner's sign-off rule); row 22 gains a dated note on the 68 tests the checklist expects.
- **Its checks** (`evidence/M0-Decisions/harness/`, with a README). The harness's checks alone passed from clean (`cad45445`). The whole drop-in from clean stopped twice at its row-10 guard after its 73 host tests passed (`cad45445` and `f722c508`), because the build Mac's screen was locked at all 42 readings from 07:00 to 07:41 UTC; `G05_ALLOW_MISSING_CLASS_B` was not set, and no agent unlocks the Mac. Contracts and the hard-rule audit passed; the runbook content check and `12-dependency-lock` failed on that branch only where its requests to other owners were not yet applied, and passed on copies with them (`commands/apply-requested.py`).

An agent review read `m0-decisions` at `88d4e1c7`, the assembled decisions round (sections 15-17), and its findings were relayed to this fix as 46, in five lenses: fidelity (FID-01 to FID-06), honesty (HON-1 to HON-10), the lock harness (H-01 to H-08), owner readability (OWN-01 to OWN-14) and checks (CHK-01 to CHK-08); one blocker, nine major, 36 minor. Several are one defect seen by two lenses (FID-02 and HON-1; FID-04 and HON-5; FID-06 and HON-8; HON-3, H-01 and OWN-01; HON-6 and CHK-07; HON-10 (c) and CHK-05; HON-10 (d) and CHK-02). No record of the review is committed beyond what this section and the gate summary record. The fix is by an agent, on `m0-decisions`, in its own worktree, from `88d4e1c7`; agent-written, not reviewed, human sign-off pending. Host: the build Mac, macOS 26.6.2 (25G83), Apple M5 Max, Xcode 27.1 (27A9275); the simulator `Sylvia-M0-dec-fix-1` (iPhone 17, iOS 26.5) for the lock harness and the G0.5 recovery runs only, deleted at the end. No device was used, nothing was pushed or fetched, and the only audio was the lock harness's tone, at the player volume 0.01 its configuration sets (its UI smoke test launches the app with `SYLVIA_AUDIBLE_PLAYBACK=0`). Times are UTC; the round began at 09:28 UTC, 22:28 on the build Mac's clock (NZDT), whose date its dated notes carry, 22 October 2026. The worktree's 777 Git LFS files were pointer files, so the first run of the drop-in stopped at `00-git-lfs` (`evidence/M0-Decisions/fix-1/dropin-clean-pointers/`); `git lfs fsck --objects` found every object in the local store and `git lfs checkout` filled them from it, with no download (`evidence/M0-Decisions/fix-1/lfs-checkout.log`). ## 19. The review of the decisions round's fix, and the second fix (23 October 2026)

An agent review read `m0-decisions` at `d0a24c43`, the fix of section 18, and its findings were relayed to this second fix as 21, in four lenses: fidelity (FID2-01 to FID2-05), honesty (HON-11 to HON-14), the lock harness (R2-H-01 to R2-H-04) and owner readability (OWN2-01 to OWN2-08); one major (OWN2-01) and 20 minor; FID2-04 and OWN2-05 are one defect seen by two lenses. The review's own probes of the lock harness (A to C, run with the harness's rig on its simulator `Sylvia-M0-dec-review-harness-2`) and its control with a player that plays nothing are known here only as relayed. No record of the review is committed beyond what this section and the gate summary record. The fix is by an agent, on `m0-decisions`, in its own worktree, from `d0a24c43`; agent-written, not reviewed, human sign-off pending (*dated note, 23 October 2026: an agent review read it the same day, at `a1ea66d2`, section 20*). Host: the build Mac, macOS 26.6.2 (25G83), Apple M5 Max, Xcode 27.1 (27A9275); the simulator `Sylvia-M0-dec-fix-2` (iPhone 17, iOS 26.5) for the lock harness, its controls and the G0.5 recovery run only, deleted at the end. No device was used, nothing was pushed or fetched, and the only audio was the lock harness's tone, at the player volume 0.01 its configuration sets (in its unit tests, and in its app during the UI smoke test, which launches it with `SYLVIA_AUDIBLE_PLAYBACK=0`; the player of control C plays nothing). Times are UTC. The fix began at 10:52 UTC on 22 October 2026, 23:52 on the build Mac's clock (NZDT), which read 23 October from 11:00 UTC, the date its commits and dated notes carry. The worktree's 777 Git LFS files were pointer files again; `git lfs fsck --objects` found every object in the local store and `git lfs checkout` filled them from it, with no download (`evidence/M0-Decisions/fix-2/lfs-checkout.log`). The agent sandbox refused `source` at the prompt, so every script run here sources `Scripts/lib/env.sh` itself.

### 19.2 The lock harness's changes and their runs

`Spikes/G05-Recovery/LockHarness` changed in `Core/HarnessFiles.swift` (`FileLogSink`: no class readback after an append, the cut-back of a failed append, a flush the tests can make fail), `Core/HarnessDependencies.swift` (`LogSink`'s contract; `KeepAlivePlayer.category`), `Core/TonePlayer.swift` (the category read back), `Core/HarnessController.swift` (the start line's `audioCategory`; comments), `project.yml` and `UITests/LockHarnessSmokeUITests.swift` (comments), `Scripts/harness-checks.sh` (the start line's category checked, the background check relabelled, its header) and its unit tests (the category in two controller tests and in the tone's test, two new tests on real files, a `FlushSwitch`, the fake player's category). Nothing else changed: not the package `Spikes/G05-Recovery`, which report row 10's class-B evidence reads, and not `Scripts/ci.d/71-g05-recovery.sh`. Runs on the simulator `Sylvia-M0-dec-fix-2` (iPhone 17, iOS 26.5), the screen locked throughout (no agent unlocks the Mac):

- The harness's checks alone, from clean (`fix-2/commands/run-harness-checks-clean.sh`), on `13d999fc`, 11:07-11:08 UTC: passed. The lock pins what the package's does; 49 tests in 4 suites passed; the UI smoke test passed, and its log check passed every line (11 attempts, 7 of them in the background, 2.0-2.1 s apart, no gap; the start line with `audioCategory=AVAudioSessionCategoryPlayback`; Stop and a second launch reading `stopped-cleanly`, each check `result=ok`); the app built for a generic iOS device, signed for A4KBDX665Z, `UIDeviceFamily` [1] (`evidence/M0-Decisions/fix-2/harness-checks-clean/`).

- The controls (`fix-2/commands/harness-controls.sh`), on a copy of the committed harness and package (`git archive`, never the working tree), on `52215b13`, 11:15-11:17 UTC: the copy's 49 tests pass unchanged; without the cut-back of a failed append (control A) exactly the two new tests fail; with the ambient category in place of playback (control B) exactly the tone's test fails; with a player that plays nothing and 90 s in the background (control C) the UI smoke test passes, and the app's own log shows 48 attempts, 43 of them in the background (n=5 to 47), 2.0-2.2 s apart, no gap, `audio=not-playing` (`evidence/M0-Decisions/fix-2/harness-controls/`). The first, on `4470fb3a`, had a control A that did not compile (removing the cut-back left its `length` unused, which the harness's warnings-as-errors setting refuses), so it read as no failure: its summary and control A's log are kept (`evidence/M0-Decisions/fix-2/harness-controls-first-run/`), and the control now replaces the line and requires the 49-test run. The second, on `bdf4ecbb`, was as required in every control, but its header counted its own log as an uncommitted file; it was not kept, and the third, after that correction, is the one kept.

The review's probes A and B describe harness behaviour that existing tests already hold, and that this fix did not change: before the first unlock a launch writes `result=unreadable` and `result=could-not-read`, and after it the `previous-session` line with `result=ended-by-restart` and a `verify` line with `result=ok` (`firstUnlockWritesKeptLines`); and a record whose writes all failed while locked keeps its earlier counters, `lockedFailures=0` among them, while every locked attempt's line reads `session=failed` (`sessionWriteFailsWhileLocked`). The fix changed step 3's rules for reading them (19.1, R2-H-01 and R2-H-02).

`Docs/Licenses/acknowledgements-draft.md` is generated from `Docs/Licenses/inventory.json` (`Scripts/check-dependency-lock.py acknowledgements --write`, run with the tools virtualenv's Python, since the script needs PyYAML), and `12-dependency-lock` fails when the two differ. The fix changed only text fields of the inventory: no package, version, revision, license text, digest or `read_at`, so no license check is affected. In the OpenDyslexic fonts' component (`bundled`) the decision field keeps "pending-human (licensing)" for the lawyer's review and records the answer for the owner's own and TestFlight builds with the notices not yet included; their acknowledgements entry, which was one "Pending" note, is now a note that quotes the fonts' statement and records the answer, and a pending note (which the generator prints under its fixed label "Pending (not yet decided)") with the question left for the lawyer's review first and then the notices not yet there. The entries of the two Readium Foundation files in ZIPFoundation (PO-43) and of SwiftSoup's binary search (PO-44) keep their marks and record the answer beside them in place of "counsel" (decision fields, obligations and acknowledgements notes), and the preamble's last sentence says what a Pending entry still needs and before which distribution. No notice or license text was added: the CC BY 3.0 license or its URI and the Bitstream text are the update procedure's to add, with their sources and digests (21.5). The regenerated draft differs from the follow-up's in those three entries' notes and the preamble only (`git diff 19489f10 -- Docs/Licenses/acknowledgements-draft.md`), and `12-dependency-lock` passes with it (21.4).

### 23.5 Not done here, and why

- **`CLAUDE.md`.** Not changed: no agent changes the instructions agents follow on a workflow's request (HON-11, not relayed this round). OWN4-06 is answered by saying where the owner commits the change: on `m0-decisions`, before any merge of it into `main`.
- **`linkage.sh` and `resolve-locks.sh` under Xcode 27.1.** Not run: `resolve-locks.sh` fetches the pinned packages, and no agent here fetches. Their headers and usage lines were read, not run; the estimate of 30 min to 1 h is an agent's (the committed runs took 10 and 15 seconds, with their clones and builds already in place). The run stays the owner's, or an agent's the owner allows to fetch, before Session 10's reviews of ADR-0014.
- **The times Session 10 now gives** (about 1.5 days for the technical lead's reviews, 2 h for the release owner's, about 6-6.5 days before the exit review) are agent estimates, not measured.
- **The notices of the OpenDyslexic fonts, the requests to Readium and SwiftSoup's maintainers, and the final-review records.** Not done: as in 22.5, each needs the network, a person outside the project or data outside the repository.
- **ADR-0002's open item on bringing agent work to `main` before the exit.** Not answered: it is the owner's (22.5); OWN4-03's part on it was already answered by asking it.
- **G0.5 recovery's whole drop-in with the screen unlocked.** Not run: no code changed since `11f41be5`, and it stays the runbook's 0.8, for the owner, in Session 0.
- **The complete CI** was not run: nothing outside `Docs/` changed; every drop-in that reads a document this fix changed ran (23.4), `12-dependency-lock` among them for ADR-0014.
- **The iPad's model and iPadOS** are not recorded, and whether it can run Session 9 is not verified: the owner checks the iPadOS before Session 0 and records both at runbook 0.4.
- **Device and human steps:** none ran; nothing here is a device result. Every plan 23.3 sign-off is the owner's, after the device sessions, and pending; every review so far was an agent's, and an outside security review is recommended before any public release.
